Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The monte_carlo variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 840 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 961 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to monte_carlo theory, we decompose the problem into 840 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 961. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
